利率上行引發波動，惟無損 AI 供應鏈擴張動能
2026 年 10 月
9 月全球股市普遍回落，主因為油價急升、全球長端殖利率攀高及主要央行轉鷹。僅有日本股市
受 AI、半導體需求與企業信心支撐，抵銷 BOJ 升息壓力，成為主要市場中唯一正報酬。標普 500 9
月小跌 0.3%，雖 AI 與大型科技股獲利韌性仍在，但 Fed 於 9 月升息並暗示年底前再度升息，加上
油價與美債殖利率走高，壓抑估值並引發科技股震盪。印度股市下跌 6.9%最弱，主要受油價飆升、
全球利率上行及外資單月淨流出約 27 億美元拖累。中國股市下跌 4.7%，因受內需修復偏弱、科技

股評價承壓所致；歐洲股市也因油價單月大漲近 14%與全球殖利率攀升影響，9 月下跌 4.6%。
9 月主要債市全面下跌，核心壓力來自全球殖利率急升。美歐日央行同步升息、Brent 原油單月
上漲約 14%、 美國經濟數據偏強與公債供給疑慮 ， 使美國10 年期殖利率單月上升約 53bp，創 2022
年以來最大升幅。主要券種中，全球投資級公司債下跌 2.93%表現最弱，新興美元債亦下跌 2.91%，
因 9 月全球公債殖利率快速攀升，此兩類債券對利率變化均較敏感 ； 其中投資級公司債相較高收益債
存續期較長、票息緩衝較低，因此在利率重新定價期間承受較大價格壓力。亞洲高收益債下挫 1.20%
相對抗跌，因高票息與較短存續期提供緩衝；新興當地債跌 1.74%，亦優於多數美元債種。
在股市方面，AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器 DRAM、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供
應鏈維持成長動能，市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分化。若AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資
本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整

體而言 ， 科技產業與台灣及日本股市獲利表現仍具關注度 ， 惟後續仍須留意全球景氣 、 產業供需變化 、
政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
就債市而言，雖然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶
來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率
環境下，中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級
公司債指數利差約 80 個基點 ， 雖然利差偏窄 ， 但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準 。 因此，
在目前市場環境下 ， 中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別 ， 惟後續表現仍須
留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

美國股市：通膨與升息預期上升推動美債利率上漲，美股呈高檔區間震盪
9 月因美伊關係緊張升溫 ， 推升油價並加劇市場對通膨回升的擔憂 ；相較於 6 月點陣圖 ，9 月FOMC
官員全面上修 2026 至 2027 年的政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示 2026 年底前會再升息一次，加上美
國經濟數據表現優於預期，使美國公債殖利率走升，進而壓抑美股表現。在此背景下，9 月標普 500
指數呈現高檔區間震盪格局。產業表現方面，資訊科技與通訊服務類股相對強勢，持續扮演指數上漲
的主要動能；相較之下，醫療保健、必需消費及部分工業類股表現較為疲弱。

9 月美國聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 的表決結果 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調25 個基點至
3.75%到 4.00%。9 月最新經濟預測，美國聯準會官員上修 2026 年核心 PCE 至 3.4%，2027 年維持
在 2.5%，將 2026-2027 年經濟成長上調 0.1%至 2.3%與 2.4%，顯示聯準會認為美國經濟與通膨仍
具韌性。
觀察美國近期的經濟數據可以發現企業投資動能依然強健，元大投顧表示，美國8 月扣除飛機與
國防的核心資本財訂單由前期的月增 0.6%加速至 1.6%， 其中初級金屬 、 機械及電機設備訂單均呈現
增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成長形
成穩健貢獻。
自 6 月底以來 ， 市場持續上修標普500 指數企業獲利預估 ， 其中以科技與能源類股貢獻最為明顯 ，
反映企業營運表現優於市場預期。AI 投資循環持續推進、企業數位轉型需求增加，以及美國內需維
持穩健，共同支撐企業獲利成長動能，並有助於吸收高利率環境帶來的壓力。與此同時，標普 500

指數前瞻本益比已由先前高點回落，評價壓力較先前明顯緩解。隨著企業獲利持續改善，美股行情正
由過去仰賴資金與情緒推升評價 ， 逐步轉向由基本面支撐股價 ， 後續表現將更取決於企業獲利是否持
續優於預期。
投資策略方面，投資焦點已逐漸由評價擴張轉向企業獲利成長與基本面表現。短期市場仍須留意
政策面不確定性 ， 美中貿易談判進展將牽動全球供應鏈與科技產業展望 ， 若關稅或科技限制措施再度
升溫，可能對科技股評價造成壓力；此外，美國期中選舉逐漸接近，企業稅率、科技監管及產業政策
等議題亦可能影響市場風險偏好。整體而言，企業獲利改善仍是支撐美股最重要的力量，若 AI 投資
循環與獲利成長趨勢延續，美股中長期表現仍可望由基本面驅動。

歐洲股市：儘管企業獲利上修，仍需持續關注地緣政治發展
9 月泛歐 600 指數下跌約 2%，產業表現方面 ，製藥 、生技、銀行及部分科技類股表現相對強勢，
顯示資金偏好具獲利能見度與防禦特性的族群 ； 相較之下 ， 礦業 、 消費及部分金融服務類股表現落後 ，
反映市場對歐洲經濟成長及企業獲利前景仍持審慎態度。整體而言，受到高油價以及高利率環境，投
資氣氛轉趨保守，市場資金持續聚焦於基本面較穩健的產業。
歐洲央行 9 月政策利率由 2.25% 升息 1 碼至 2.50 % ，符合市場預期。歐洲央行最新經濟預
測中上修中期通膨展望，2027 年消費者物價指數年增率由 2.3% 升至 2.5% ，2028 年則由 2.0%

上調至 2.1% ；核心消費者物價指數方面，2027 年和 2028 年預測亦各上修 0.1 % 至 2.6% 及
2.3% ，能源成本上升對食品及其他價格的傳導，使得通膨回落速度較原先預期緩慢。ECB 總裁拉加
德表示能源價格上漲逐步傳導至核心及食品通膨，預期整體通膨在 2027 年上半年維持在高檔。
根據花旗歐洲獲利修正指數(Earnings Revision Index) ，歐洲企業獲利預估已連續上修並到近五
年高點，反映企業基本面持續改善。受惠於歐元區景氣穩步復甦、製造業景氣回升，以及企業資本支
出逐步回暖，工業、金融及景氣循環類股獲利展望同步改善。隨著市場對企業未來盈餘預期提高，有
助支撐歐股評價面與資金流入，並成為推動泛歐 600 指數表現的重要動能。
然而，自 3 月美伊衝突爆發以來，市場對能源供給中斷的擔憂持續升溫，帶動油價走高，進而增
加仰賴進口能源的歐洲企業成本壓力，並壓抑歐股相對表現。進入9 月後，俄羅斯與北約緊張關係再
度升溫，歐洲安全局勢不確定性提高，進一步加劇市場對能源供應與地緣政治風險的擔憂。若相關衝

突持續延燒，不僅可能推升能源價格與通膨壓力，也可能影響企業投資意願及市場風險偏好，進而對
歐洲企業獲利與股市表現形成壓力。
展望 10 月，歐股仍將受到高油價、地緣政治風險及歐洲央行維持偏鷹立場等因素影響，市場波
動性可能維持偏高。近期能源價格攀升推升通膨預期，使 ECB 傾向維持較高利率水準，不僅提高企
業融資成本，也限制股市評價進一步擴張的空間。此外，中東局勢持續緊張，以及俄羅斯與北約間的
緊張關係再度升高，均可能影響投資人風險偏好，增添歐洲金融市場不確定性。雖然歐元區經濟活動
仍維持溫和擴張，企業獲利預估亦持續改善，但在高利率與地緣政治風險尚未明顯緩解之前，市場情
緒仍偏向審慎，後續仍需持續關注能源價格、通膨走勢及地緣政治發展，作為判斷歐股能否重啟上漲
動能的重要觀察指標。

日本股市：升息落地，景氣展望佳，第四季半導體與商社企業獲利動能有望延續
9 月日本股市受升息預期及長天期公債殖利率走高影響，部分高評價股票出現震盪，但這波調整
較偏向政策轉折下的評價重估，而非企業基本面全面轉弱。日本銀行於 9 月 18 日決議將短期政策利
率目標提高至約 1.25%，使市場反覆討論的升息方向逐漸明朗。隨著政策落地，投資焦點有望由利率
變動轉回企業營運與獲利。從正面角度看，利率正常化反映日本經濟具備一定韌性，股價整理也有助
於消化先前累積的評價壓力。若後續企業財測保持穩健，9 月的震盪可望成為第四季行情重新蓄勢的

過程。
日本股市的中期支撐，來自薪資成長、內需改善與企業投資的相互帶動。2026 年春鬥最終統計
顯示，5,368 個工會的平均調薪幅度達 5.01%，連續第三年維持在 5%以上；可明確辨識的基本薪資
調升幅度亦達 3.50%。薪資持續增加，有助於提升家庭消費能力，為零售與服務等內需產業提供較穩
定的需求基礎。若企業能透過產品競爭力、定價能力及經營效率吸收成本，消費回溫便有機會轉化為
獲利成長。同時，全球 AI 相關投資也為日本半導體設備、載板及電子零組件供應鏈創造機會，使內
需復甦與科技需求形成雙重支撐。
展望 10 月，隨著升息議題逐步由預期轉為已知條件，市場可望更加重視第四季訂單、企業財測
及實際獲利。科技產業方面，若全球 AI 相關資本支出持續擴大，日本具技術優勢的設備與零組件企
業，有機會將需求轉化為接單及出貨成長；商社與資源相關企業，則可觀察能源、原物料業務及投資
收益對現金流的貢獻。內需產業亦值得留意薪資成長能否帶動消費，以及企業能否維持利潤率。整體
而言，10 月的投資機會不必侷限於單一題材，市場有望逐步從政策消息主導，轉向由產業需求與企

業業績支撐的行情。
投資策略方面，半導體供應鏈可優先挑選訂單能見度高、具技術門檻且獲利成長明確的企業；商
社則著重現金流、資本配置與股東回饋，內需股則觀察消費回溫能否反映在營收及利潤。若股價因利
率或匯率波動而短暫回落，但企業基本面與財測未明顯改變，可考慮分批布局，避免追逐短線漲幅。
後續仍須追蹤長天期利率 、 日圓及企業財測變化 ， 但投資主軸可更積極地放在具備實質成長能力的公
司。日本央行升息落地後，企業獲利能否接棒，將是日本股市延續表現的關鍵。

台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產 ， 則為自動化設備 、 銅箔及高階材料創造機會 。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨 、 自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進 ， 相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件 。 與此同時 ， 低價及開放權重策略持續降低AI 使用門檻 ， 帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月 ， 中國AI 的成長空間仍在 ， 但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模 ， 逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善 。 晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要 ， 價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用 ， 讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值 。 光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸 ， 也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展 ， 拓展供應商的產品空間 。Lumentum 指出 ，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升 ， 帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長 。 高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進 。 雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。 「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求 。 配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心 ， 再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程 ， 入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠 ； 選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理 。 一方面 ， 拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期 ，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力 ， 比市場原先擔心的情況更好 。 其中一個重要原因是 ， 新興市場政府透過燃油補貼 、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變 ， 並將2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%， 低於6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點 ，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策 ， 為巴西央行創造更多降息空間 ， 有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美 各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外 ，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響 ，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶 ，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外 ，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復 ，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升 ， 帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油 ， 轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運 ， 帶動拉斯坦努拉港9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修 ， 且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升 ，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

### Page 10

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點 ， 加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金ETF 需
求下滑 ， 高盛因此將2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元 。 然而 ， 高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪 。 然而 ， 全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金 ， 加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置 ，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

### Page 11

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過 ， 將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響 ， 美國經濟仍維持強勁成長 ，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮 ，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量 ， 投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素 ，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降 ， 存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響 。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債 。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 歐洲央行上周四(9/10)為年內第二次上調利率，以應對能源價格上漲帶來的通膨
壓力 ， 並警告物價壓力可能比先前預期更久 ， 推動市場加大對後續進一步收緊政
策的押注 。 歐洲央行總裁拉加德形容此次决定爲”無需多想的决定”， 並警告通膨
回落至 2%目標的時間點目前預計在 2027 年底。歐洲央行預期明年平均通膨爲
2.5%，高於 6 月的 2.3%；2027 年核心通膨預期爲 2.6%，高於此前的 2.5%。
⚫ 軍事消息人士稱，與伊朗結盟的胡塞武裝上周四(9/10)奪取葉門港口城市的控制
權 ， 並沿紅海海岸向南推進至戰略島嶼地區 。 美國總統川普表示 ， 他預計伊朗戰
爭將在美國中期選舉後結束。若胡塞武裝控制這條與 荷姆茲海峽同樣重要的水
道 ， 其支持者伊朗將在與美國的戰爭中獲得關鍵戰略優勢 ， 並進一步推高油價 。
⚫ 美國勞工部勞工統計局上週四(9/10)公布 8 月生産者物價指數 PPI 年增 5.4%，

高於市場預期的 5.3％。週五(9/11)公布 8 月消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 3.4%與前
月相同，核心 CPI 年增率 2.4%符合市場預期，月增率達 0.3%高於市場預期。
FED WATCH顯示數據公布後推升市場對於聯準會本周將升息的預期。
⚫ 日本央行數據顯示，8 月國內企業物價指數較去年同期上漲 7.6%。增幅高於市
場預估中值 7.4%。7 月漲幅經修正後爲 7.7%；8 月進口物價指數同比上漲 24.8%，
7 月修正後爲上漲 29.3%， 表明當月日圓貶值以及中東局勢影響推高進口成本和
整體通膨，同時鞏固市場對日本央行在 9 月會議進一步升息預期。

貶值 0.8 分。

走升帶動美元指數跌破 99 整數價位低檔震盪 ， 加以台股高檔震盪 ， 外資延續匯入的
走勢，帶動台幣升破31.500 至 31.455 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月生產者物
價指數年增率由上個月的 4.8%上升至 5.4%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示聯準會
9 月升息機率攀升至 7 成，美元指數回到 99 上方，台股高點回落，外資偏向匯出，
台幣由升轉貶至 31.698 週內低點，終場在央行調節之下收在 31.638。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣區間震盪。國際市場方面，上週五公布美國 8 月核心消費者物價
指數月增率 0.3%高於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，提高本週聯準會升息的機率

至接近 9 成，此外中東局勢仍有升溫風險，油價持續高檔震盪，美元指數在 99 整數
關口盤整；就台灣市場來看，台灣 8 月出口衝上 824 億美元，創下歷年單月新高紀
錄，月增 9.4%，年增 41%，展現極為強勁的成長動能。顯示受惠於 AI 需求全面爆
發，帶動外銷出口暢旺，基本面強勁持續支撐台幣的表現；盤勢上，匯價逢低吸引
進口商及政府基金買盤進場，逢高則面臨出口商賣美金，且本週台美均有利率決策
會議，預估匯率波動將加劇。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢區間震盪。

美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/14/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週人民幣震盪走升。週初，雖然美伊戰火未平，帶動油價及美債利率高漲，
但市場對於日本央行 9 月升息預期升溫，日圓偏升帶動美元指數跌破 99 價位後低
檔震盪，人民幣延續偏升走勢，走升至6.7022 週內高點；臨近週末，美國公布8 月
生產者物價指數 5.4%優於市場預期 5.3%， 加以8 月核心消費者物價指數月增率 0.3%
優於市場預期的 0.2%，數據公布後，FEDWATCH顯示本週聯準會 9 月升息預期大
幅攀升至近 9 成，美元指數回到99 上方，人民幣回吐部分週初以來的漲幅，終場境
外人民幣收在 6.7071，週線升值 8 點，境內人民幣則收在6.7079，週線升值 29 點。  
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間偏升。國際市場方面，美國通膨數據表現優於市場預期，
加以油價攀升至每桶 100 美元上方 ， 美債殖利率位居高檔 ， 美元指數也在99 上方盤
整，關注本週聯準會最新利率決議，預計在會議結果出爐以前，聯準會升息預期將
支撐美元指數，限制人民幣升值的空間。中國國內方面，人行中間價格緩步偏升，
上週升破 6.7800 價位，持續釋放人民幣偏強信號，支撐人民幣延續升值的方向。技
術面方面 ，USD/CNH 匯價居於各天期均線下方 ，且均線向下發散 ，加以匯價由低點
向上反彈測試 6.7150 關鍵價位後面臨壓力，顯示空方動能較盛，預計人民幣將延續
升值的態勢。綜合以上，預估本週人民幣走勢區間偏升。

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 震盪下跌 。週初 ，因市場預期日本央行將加快升息步伐及資金將
回流日本，使 USD/JPY 跌破至 155 整數關卡之下；週間，USD/JPY 延續跌勢最低
來到 152.87 後反彈，後續大致在 153 至 154 間波動；臨近週末，美國公布 8 月 PPI
及 CPI 數據顯示通膨仍處高位 ，USD/JPY 一度反彈至154.6 後回落 ， 終場收在153.54，
週線大跌 270 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、上週公布日本第二季 GDP
較上季增長 0.4%，季增年率也由 1.1%上升至 1.4%，主因為企業資本支出改善，而
7 月名目薪資年增 4.7%為 30 年來最大漲幅，而實質薪資年增 2.4%也為 5 年多來最
大漲幅，顯示日本經濟復甦力道較原先預期強勁，進一步強化市場對於日央升息的
預期，有利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月 CPI 及 PPI 數據顯示美國通膨仍處高位，強化
市場對聯準會升息預期，且中東局勢升溫，帶動油價突破 100 美元，預期將支撐美
元，限縮 USD/JPY 下行空間。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價多次向
下測試 153 整數關卡未能有效突破，顯示下方有買盤支撐，而 155 關卡附近則為前
波壓力位，因此短線先以區間看待。綜合以上，預估USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的
可能性較高。

展現韌性，預期第三季仍會延續，但未來通膨仍面臨上行風險，偏鷹派言論使市場
對於歐洲央行未來持續升息的預期上升，將帶給歐元支撐。二、美國 8 月 PPI 月增
0.4%符合市場預期 ， 年增5.4%高於預期 ，而 8 月 CPI 月增 0.4%年增 3.4%符合預期 ，
顯示通膨仍維持高位，使市場對聯準會升息預期增加，將支撐美元，不利歐元匯價
上行。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價雖一度突破 1.1630 年線但未
能站穩，顯示上方仍有賣壓，而下方1.1550 附近則有支撐，在有效突破前以區間震
盪看待。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (11 5)金管投顧新字第 016 號         Page 2 of 5 
個股報告 
禾 榮 科 核 心 技 術 為 自 主 研 發
的 AB -BNCT 癌症治療設備  
 禾榮科成立於 2017 年，專注於發展先進的癌症治療技術，公司核心業務為加
速器型硼中子捕獲治療（Accelerator-Based Boron Neut ron Capture Therapy, 
AB-BNCT），此項技術源自清華大學與工業技術研究院歷經三十年的研究成
果，禾榮科成功將這項學研基礎轉化為商業應用，提供完整的 AB-BNCT 整體
解決方案，涵蓋質子加速器系統、中子源靶系統、中子束調整器、治療控制系
統、治療計畫系統以及病人定位系統等關鍵設備，致力於為醫療機構與患者帶
來精準、高效的治療選擇。

B10 L -BPA 治 療 藥 物 預 計
2026 年底取得台灣藥證  
 禾榮科技 AB-BNCT 設備已於 2024 年 7 月正式取得 TFDA 醫療器材許可證，其
核心治療新藥 B10 L-BPA (10B-L-Boronophenylalanine)正進行復發型頭頸癌、
惡性腦瘤及復發型腦膜瘤的臨床試驗，復發型頭頸癌收案 24 人，主要評估指標
為治療後三個月的客觀反應率 （ORR） ，預計2026 年底取得藥證，目前禾榮科
營收仍主要來自恩慈治療，取得藥證後，將可正式收治病患，且未來將擴大適
應症至三陰性乳癌與肺腺癌等；此外，搭配術前精準評估劑量的 F18 FBPA 診
斷用藥已於 4Q25 進入臨床試驗。

禾 榮 科 獲 利 來 自 「 設 備 銷
售 、 設 備 維 保 、 治 療 藥 物 、
診斷藥物」四大支柱  
 在設備銷售方面，禾榮科技提供買斷、租賃與分潤等多元商業模式， AB-BNCT
系統單台售價約為新台幣 12 至 16 億元，設備售出後每年可穩定收取設備售價
5%至 9%的維修保養費用，若採分潤模式，BNCT 治療費用為 60 萬元，加藥費
為 100 萬元，禾榮科可抽取治療費用的 30%至 40%作為收益。

在藥物佈局上，核心治療藥物 BPA 平均每人次收費為新台幣 40 萬元，目前於
臨床試驗與恩慈治療中使用，待取得藥證後即可獨立進行商業化銷售；而診斷
藥物 FBPA 每次費用約新台幣 4 至 8 萬元，且其用量遠大於治療藥物。

2027 年 開 始 收 治病患，且
認 列 一 台 設 備 ， 預 估EPS 
6.29 元  
 展望 2027 年，預估營收 23 億元，中醫大 1Q27 開始貢獻營收，北榮 2Q27 開
始貢獻營收，預估 2027 年藥費與治療分潤貢獻營收 11 億元，設備銷售認列營
收 12 億元；藥費與分潤毛利率超過 80%，設備銷售毛利率超過 50%，預估
2027 年毛利率 65%，預估 2027 年 EPS 6.29 元。

維持 買進評等 ，目標價 700
元  
 禾榮科治療藥物即將取得許可證，屆時即可正式開始收費，同時， 1H27 將認
列台北榮總 BNCT 設備 12 億元營收，2027 年開始轉虧為盈，未來隨著適應症
擴展及海外布局，營收與獲利將爆發成長，禾榮科投資評等維持買進，目標價
700 元。

## Document 110: 9月美国CPI数据点评：通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月美国CPI数据点评：通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动.pdf
Report source: 交银国际证券
Date: 2026-10-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

此报告最后部分的分析师披露、商业关系披露和免责声明为报告的一部分，必须阅读。
下载本公司之研究报告，可从彭博搜寻NH BCM  或 登录研究部网站https://research.bocomgroup.com 
交银国际研究 
宏观策略

2026年10月16日
全球宏观 
通胀超预期之下，加息预期剧烈波动——9月美国CPI数据点评
美国通胀回升，核心通胀超预期，核心价格压力有所扩散。9月美国CPI环比
上涨0.6%，较8月的0.4%进一步加速，同比由3.4%升至3.7%，高于市场预期
的3.6%；核心CPI环比上涨0.3%，与8月持平，高于预期的0.2%，同比由8
月的2.4%升至2.6%。总体通胀回升主要由能源推动，但核心通胀环比上涨显
示，通胀回落进程面临新的阻力。
食品通胀整体温和，能源继续成为推升总通胀的主要力量。 食品价格环比持
平、同比上涨2.5%，其中家庭食品下降0.1%，外出就餐上涨0.2%。能源价格
环比上涨5.2%、同比上涨20.4%，其中汽油环比大涨9.1%、同比上涨34.2%，
贡献当月CPI涨幅的一半以上；燃油价格环比上涨15.3%。中东地缘冲突导致
能源供应持续受扰，汽柴油价格高企不仅直接推升通胀，还可能通过运输及
生产成本向其他商品价格传导。
核心商品通胀有所加速，核心服务通胀粘性增强。核心商品环比上涨0.3%，
高于8月的0.1%，同比上涨0.7%。其中二手车价格上涨0.7%，新车价格上涨
0.2%，服装上涨0.3%，医疗护理商品则下降0.7%。核心服务环比由0.3%升至
0.4%、同比上涨3.1%。其中住房环比上涨0.4%，业主等价租金上涨0.3%，住
宿上涨2.0%；交通服务上涨0.6%，航空票价上涨1.2%、同比涨22.4%；汽车
维修、医院服务分别上涨0.7%，教育上涨0.6%。住房仍是核心通胀的重要支
撑，而交通、教育及部分医疗服务价格上涨，显示剔除住房后的服务通胀压
力也不容忽视。
展望未来，能源冲击与服务价格粘性或使通胀在年底前维持高位。10月汽柴
油价格仍是最大不确定性，其向运输、食品和商品价格的传导可能尚未结束。
到年底，若油价维持高位，总体CPI同比或持续高于3%，核心通胀回落也将
较为缓慢。进入2027年，若中东能源供应改善、住房租金滞后回落及工资增
速放缓，通胀有望重新进入下降通道；反之，能源与关税冲击的持续传导可
能使通胀高位运行更长时间。
美联储政策方面，通胀超预期推动美联储进一步加息的概率剧烈波动。 数据
公布前，市场对10月加息25基点的定价约为两成，公布后一度升至45%—
50%，随后回落到约两成多的水平。我们认为，考虑到能源供给冲击无法通
过加息直接解决，叠加就业增长有所放缓，美联储仍可能选择10月暂时观
望，但通胀二轮传导风险有所上升，12月加息仍是基准情形。
市场反应：呈现股市下跌、美元走强的特征。 CPI公布当日收盘，道指、标普
500和纳指全线下跌，分别下跌约0.09%、1.11%和1.88%，科技股特别是半导
体承压明显，金融股受银行财报影响表现分化，能源相关资产则受到高油价
支撑。2年期美债收益率冲高回落到收盘上行约3基点，10年期美债收益率盘
中上升，但收盘下降2个基点；美元指数上涨约0.04%；黄金下跌约1.6%；布
伦特原油维持在每桶106美元附近。整体来看，市场重新定价美联储加息路
径，利率敏感型资产面临压力。


王博群, PhD, FRM
hugh.wang@bocomgroup.com

### Page 6

评级定义
分析员个股评级定义 : 分析员行业评级定义：
买入：预期个股未来12个月的总回报高于相关行业。
中性：预期个股未来12个月的总回报与相关行业一致。
沽出：预期个股未来12个月的总回报低于相关行业
无评级：对于个股未来12个月的总回报与相关行业的比较，
分析员并无确信观点。
领先：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现相对于大盘
标杆指数具吸引力。
同步：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现与大盘标杆
指数一致。
落后：分析员预期所覆盖行业未来12个月的表现相对于大盘
标杆指数不具吸引力。
香港市场的标杆指数为恒生综合指数，A股市场的标杆指数
为MSCI中国A股指数，美国上市中概股的标杆指数为标普美
国中概股50（美元）指数

公司的高级人员（包括就房地产基金而言，担任该房地产基金的管理公司的高级人员；及就任何其他实体而言，在该实体中担任负责管理该
等公司的高级人员或其同级人员）；iii）他们及他们之相关有联系者并没拥有于本报告内涉及其评论的任何公司的证券之任何财务利益。
根据证监会持牌人或注册人操守准则第16.2段，“有联系者”指：i）分析员的配偶、亲生或领养的未成年子女，或未成年继子女；ii）某
信托的受托人，而分析员、其配偶、其亲生或领养的未成年子女或其未成年继子女是该信托的受益人或酌情对象；或iii）惯于或有义务按

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속LG에너지솔루션(373220) 원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속원-_fba73583.pdf

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다. 
Buy (Maintain)

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 216: CTCP Nhựa Bình Minh - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/CTCP Nhựa Bình Minh - Báo cáo cập nhật 2Q2026 (2026-09-23).pdf
Report source: KBSV (Company)
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Doanh thu 2Q2026 tăng trưởng chậm 
nhưng biên lợi nhuận gộp vẫn được 
duy trì do tăng giá bán

Sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 chững lại 
do tâm lý nhập hàng thận trọng và 
hoạt động xây dựng phục hồi chậm

Biên lợi nhuận gộp được dự báo sẽ 
thu hẹp cuối năm, phản ánh chi phí 
nguyên liệu cao hơn trong 1Q2026

Khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá mục 
tiêu 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Doanh thu/LNST 2Q2026 đạt lần lượt 1,320/367 tỷ VND (+1%/+11% YoY). Lũy kế 
6M2026, doanh thu đạt 2,777 tỷ VND (+3% YoY) trong khi LNST ước đạt 671 tỷ VND 
(+9% YoY). Biên lãi gộp 6M2026 cải thiện đạt 47.0% (+240 bps YoY), chủ yếu do việc 
tăng giá bán giúp doanh nghiệp bù đắp được mức tăng của chi phí đầu vào.

Lũy kế 6M2026, sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck. Việc 
BMP thu hẹp các chương trình chiết khấu, với mức chiết khấu bình quân khoảng 
5.2% trong 2Q2026, làm giảm động lực nhập hàng của các đại lý và khiến sản lượng 
tiêu thụ chậm lại. Dù vậy, thị phần toàn quốc của BMP ước đạt 28% (+1 đpt svck) 
nhờ các doanh nghiệp khác trong ngành gặp khó khăn khi giá hạt nhựa tăng mạnh.

Biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP dự kiến thu hẹp trong 2H2026 trước áp lực chi phí 
nguyên liệu đầu vào tăng. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi kỳ vọng mức độ ảnh hưởng sẽ thấp 
hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành do (1) các lô nguyên liệu giá cao từ 
1Q2026 đã được đưa vào sản xuất và tiêu thụ một phần trong 1H2026; (2) BMP chủ 
động nâng gấp đôi hàng tồn kho để ứng phó với biến động bất lợi của giá nguyên 
liệu; (3) hơn 90% nhu cầu nguyên liệu uPVC được cung ứng từ các đối tác trong 
nước như hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam, AGC Chemicals và LSP; và (4) công ty duy 
trì giá bán bình quân ở mức cao.

Dựa trên kết quả kinh doanh và định giá, chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP với cổ 
phiếu BMP, giá mục tiêu là 166,100 VND/cổ phiếu (tương ứng với mức tăng 14.3% 
so với giá đóng cửa ngày 23/09/2026).

### Page 2

Tăng trưởng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thuần 5 năm gần nhất Hoạt động kinh doanh

Nhựa Bình Minh (BMP) là doanh nghiệp hàng đầu 
trong lĩnh vực sản xuất ống nhựa với thị phần 
dẫn đầu tại thị trường nội địa (27% vào cuối 
2023), thị trường chính tập trung tại khu vực 
miền Nam. BMP có 4 nhà máy với tổng công suất 
đạt 150,000 tấn/năm. 
Nguồn: BMP, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Kỳ vọng đà phục hồi của xây dựng dân dụng và bất động sản miền Nam sẽ thúc đẩy sản lượng tiêu 
thụ tăng trưởng trong 2026–2027. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Giá PVC được kỳ vọng sẽ hạ nhiệt và khả năng giữ giá bán đầu ra ở mức cao giúp BMP giữ biên lợi 
nhuận gộp ở nền cao. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

### Page 4

4

Nhu cầu nhập hàng thận trọng và 
cắt giảm các đợt chiết khấu trong 
kỳ của BMP làm giảm sản lượng tiêu 
thụ trong 2Q2026

Sự hồi phục của nhu cầu xây dựng 
dân dụng và nguồn cung các dự án 
bất động sản tại miền Nam sẽ thúc 
đẩy tăng trưởng sản lượng 2H2026 
 Sản lượng tiêu thụ trong 2Q2026 ước đạt 20,100 tấn, giảm 11% svck, nâng tổng 
sản lượng tiêu thụ 6M2026 ước đạt 43,920 tấn, giảm 5% svck do các yếu tố bất 
lợi từ bên ngoài như (1) giá hạt nhựa tăng cao khiến thị trường dè chừng trong 
việc nhập hàng vì lo ngại nguồn cung bất ổn (2) BMP đã thu hẹp các chương 
trình chiết khấu so với 2025 ngay trước thời điểm xung đột bùng nổ, giúp tỷ lệ 
chi phí bán hàng/doanh thu về mức 11.3%, giảm 800 bps so với 1Q2026 ở mức 
19.3%, nhưng đồng thời cũng khiến sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP suy giảm do 
chính sách bán hàng thắt chặt hơn với các đối thủ có thị phần thấp hơn.

Chúng tôi kỳ vọng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP dần hồi phục từ 3Q2026 khi nhu 
cầu nhập hàng của các đại lý cải thiện sau giai đoạn thận trọng trong nửa đầu 
năm. Về cuối năm, hoạt động xây dựng dự kiến tăng tốc khi điều kiện thi công 
thuận lợi hơn sau mùa mưa. Bên cạnh đó, việc triển khai các dự án nhà ở tại 
TP.HCM và các tỉnh lân cận được kỳ vọng hỗ trợ nhu cầu ống nhựa trong giai 
đoạn tới. Theo đó, chúng tôi dự phóng sản lượng tiêu thụ của BMP lần lượt đạt 
96,695/100,125/96,750 tấn trong 2026/2027/2028.

Biểu đồ 3. Tương quan giữa tỷ lệ chi phí bán hàng/doanh 
thu và lợi nhuận thuần/sản lượng tiêu thụ

### Page 5

5

Lợi thế từ hệ sinh thái công ty mẹ 
SCG và đối tác Nhật Bản giúp BMP 
có nhiều dư địa để nâng cao hiệu 
quả hoạt động

Theo chia sẻ từ ban lãnh đạo, BMP có lợi thế vì nhập nguyên liệu từ công ty mẹ 
là hệ sinh thái SCG tại Việt Nam và đối tác Nhật Bản là AGC Chemicals Vietnam, 
giúp giảm được 5% thuế nhập khẩu và chi phí vận chuyển. Điều này giúp BMP 
hưởng lợi kép khi có thể tăng được giá bán theo thị trường nhưng nguyên vật 
liệu rẻ hơn so với các doanh nghiệp trong ngành. KBSV nhận thấy định hướng 
dài hạn của BMP là cân bằng lợi ích cổ đông và khách hàng, vì vậy việc giảm giá 
bán để giành thị phần không phải lựa chọn ưu tiên của doanh nghiệp mà là 
nâng cao tỷ suất lợi nhuận/sản phẩm thành phẩm.

KBSV dự báo biên lợi nhuận gộp thu hẹp trong 2H2026 khi chi phí nguyên liệu 
giá cao từ 1H2026 được phản ánh vào giá vốn, và hồi phục trong 2027–2028 
nhờ đặc tính giữ giá bán ít thay đổi của nhóm nhựa. Chúng tôi kỳ vọng giá PVC 
resin chịu áp lực giảm từ (1) nhu cầu xây dựng tại Trung Quốc còn yếu khi thị 
trường bất động sản chưa hồi phục rõ ràng; và (2) các nhà sản xuất giải phóng 
hàng tồn kho sau mùa mưa, làm tăng sản lượng hạt nhựa PVC resin. Theo đó, 
KBSV dự phóng biên lợi nhuận gộp của BMP lên 46.4%/46.7%/46.5% trong 
2026/2027/2028.

## Document 258: Inflation Tracker - October 2026 _ Energy pushes headline inflation higher, core measures are still contained (2026-10-16)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Inflation Tracker - October 2026 _ Energy pushes headline inflation higher, core measures are still contained (2026-10-16).pdf

### Page 1

INFLATION TRACKER:
ENERGY PUSHES HEADLINE INFLATION HIGHER, CORE MEASURES
ARE STILL CONTAINED
October 2026

### Page 2

Key takeaways
2ECONOMIC RESEARCH
1 A rise in inflation still confined to energy
Rising energy prices are pushing
inflation higher in Europe and the
United States, while energy’s
contribution is slightly negative in
Japan.
3 Mounting pressure on producer prices
2 Some recent pressure on core measures
4 Rising short-termmarket expectations
Markets (swaps) are pricing this
rise in inflation into short-term
measures. Long-term expectations
(5y5y) are rising in Japan and the

UK.
Over recent months (3m/3m), core
indices have been firming in the
euro area and Japan: a signal to
watch.
In Europe and Japan, pressure on
producer prices is building. The
risk of the energy shock spilling
over to other CPI items is
increasing.

## Document 279: new era of chip testing 20261008 ubs

Source PDF: all report pdf/new era of chip testing 20261008 ubs.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 8 October 2026
Global Research
Asia Semiconductor Insight
AI put to the test: the new era of chip testing
Time to revisit semiconductor testing as content growth accelerates
Our core thesis is that AI-driven chip complexity is drastically increasing testing content
across wafer, package, and system levels, creating a multi-year growth opportunity that
the market has yet to fully price in. Testing companies in Taiwan have underperformed
TAIEX in the past three months (indexing at -9% vs TAIEX +7%), yet to recover from the

July market deleveraging. Investor hesitation currently stems from 1) fear of test content
growth expectations already priced-in, 2) lingering concerns over shorter test times, and
3) near-term capacity constraints among major suppliers of probe cards, load boards,
socket pins, testers, and handlers, caused by heightened testing demand. Ultimately,
these concerns overshadow the structural increase in test insertions required by AI
accelerators, chiplets, and advanced packaging. As advanced packaging scales, testing

intensity must rise in tandem, driving growth through higher content per device rather
than semiconductor unit growth alone.
Expanding testing opportunities with shift-left for CP and increasing FT content
Chip probing (CP) supply chain should be the key beneficiary of the industry's shift-left
testing trend, as more test insertions move to the wafer level before packaging, in
addition to longer CP time. We believe investors underestimate this trend, which should

accelerate with broader adoption of 2.5D/3D packaging, chiplets and larger AI
packages, driving demand for high pin count probe cards, probers, and thermal-control
equipment. We expect strong growth for Hermes Testing (HTSI)  as thermal-
management requirements rise with increasing chip power and complexity, with prober
thermal-control module shipments reaching 3,000/4,100 units in 2027E/28E, up from
1,200 in 2026E . We forecast Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT) 's sales to exceed

consensus by 7%/15% in 2027E/28E, supported by structural AI/HPC testing demand
and rising value-added content. Importantly, shift-left testing does not diminish the role
of final test (FT) and mission-mode testing. Test content and test time should continue to
increase as validation becomes increasingly critical for complex, large-form-factor AI
accelerators. We expect larger package sizes and rising system-level testing
requirements to drive demand for WinWay's high-pin-count sockets, supporting

74%/71% YoY sales growth in 2027E/28E.
Future of testing: co-packaged optics (CPO) will be a major opportunity
CPO testing represents a significant new market opportunity, though we expect the
ecosystem to remain fragmented rather than consolidating around a single winner. As AI
scaling shifts the industry bottleneck from memory to interconnect, CPO adoption is
becoming increasingly important, creating new testing challenges that will require
collaboration across equipment, optics, and test solution providers. Leveraging

ficonTEC's optical alignment expertise and HTSI's probe, testing, and manufacturing
capabilities, we estimate the HTSI  will deliver 40 units of insertion 2 equipment and 70
units of insertion 3 testing equipment in 2027 , with further upside in 2028 as CPO
deployment expands from scale-out towards scale-up.
Initiate Hermes Testing, Chunghwa Precision Test, WinWay with Buy ratings
We initiate coverage on semiconductor testing solution provider Hermes Testing with

a Buy rating,  NT$8,880 price target based on 31x 2028E PE, for thermal control/CPO
with the most attractive risk/reward in our testing coverage; test interface PCB supplier
CHPT with a Buy rating, NT$5,000 price target based on 31x avg 2027-28E PE, for
CPU/TPU programs and test board content gain; and semiconductor test socket leader
WinWay with a Buy rating, NT$8,900 price target based on 30x avg 2027-28E PE,
for socket content growth and margin recovery. Among our testing coverage, we also

including information on the Quantitative Research Review published by UBS, begin on page 22.

### Page 2

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 2
Asia Semiconductor Insight UBS Research
Sector Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP MOST FAVORED
HTSI, CHPT, WinWay, Hon Precision, Chroma ATE
LEAST FAVORED
N/A
Q: Is chip probing becoming increasingly important in the testing supply chain?
Yes.   As package costs rise, foundries must improve known-good-die (KGD) screening before
packaging, increasing demand for wafer-level testing. This should drive demand for probe cards,

probers and advanced thermal control solutions capable of managing higher power consumption,
wafer warpage and tighter test tolerances. We forecast HTSI/CHPT/WinWay' probe card business to
see a 31%/35%/33% sales CAGR for 2026-2031E, as they are positioned to grow strongly given
their smaller scale and robust growth drivers from shift left, CPU, AI accelerators. We forecast HTSI to
ship 1,200/3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2026E/27E/28E, as advanced wafer-level test for

AI accelerators will require thermal control solutions in order to prevent probe card needle burnt.
Q: Will FT and SLT content continue to grow in the coming years?
Yes. Despite concerns that final-test (FT) time could flatten or decline with Nvidia's Rubin GPUs, we
expect overall FT content to continue growing as advanced packaging complexity and chip sizes
increase. We also expect broader adoption of system-level testing (SLT)/mission-mode testing, as AI

accelerators require increasingly stringent validation to ensure optimal system and rack-level
performance. We forecast WinWay's socket business to see a 33% sales CAGR for 2026-2031E.
Q: Will CPO testing be a revenue generator in 2027-28 to supply chain players?
Yes. Testing remains a key bottleneck, creating a new equipment opportunity beginning in 2027E.
We expect initial CPO volumes in 2027, followed by broader adoption in 2028. Testing remains one

of the key bottlenecks to commercialization, driving demand for new equipment and test solutions.
We believe early leaders such as ficonTEC (partnership with HTSI) are well positioned to benefit from
the initial ramp, alongside suppliers including Chroma ATE and Hon Precision.
1) We believe upside from agentic AI CPU demand is not reflected in CHPT and WinWay shares. We
estimate CPU-related demand could contribute 20-30% of 2027E sales  for CHPT and WinWay,

respectively, with further upside in 2028E. 2) We believe market expectations for 2027 CPO testing
equipment shipments remain conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on our
industry analysis, we estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, this
should be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3 shipment
due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands.

Testing is becoming one of the largest incremental beneficiaries of AI-driven semiconductor
complexity. As more test insertions are required across wafer, package and system levels, testing
content per device should increase materially, expanding the industry's addressable market and
supporting multi-year growth across the supply chain.
TSMC and OSATs have been expanding their CoWoS capacities aggressively, from 40kwpm in 2024
to around 90 kwpm in 2025, and tracking to expand CoWoS capacity toward 160kwpm  in 2026E.

TSMC also ramped up expanding N3/N2 capacity, with multiple product launches from customers'
pipelines—Nvidia's Rubin, AMD's MI400 series/Venice CPU and other cloud ASICs—translating into
foreseeable, strong testing demand.

Executive Summary

Source: UBS estimates
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing
With the industry increasingly adopting shift-left testing, chip probing (CP) and its supply
chain should be key beneficiaries of a multi-year structural growth trend. This underpins

our above-consensus forecasts for Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT), with 2027E/28E
sales 7%/15% above consensus driven by stronger AI/HPC demand, capacity expansion,
and rising value-added content. WinWay should also benefit through its partnership
with Technoprobe, with probe card revenue growth remaining robust at 94%/72% in
2026E/27E following 161%/260% growth in 2024/25.
Hermes Testing (HTSI) should be another major beneficiary as increasing chip power

and complexity drive demand for advanced thermal-management modules used
alongside high-end probing systems. With TEL's leading AI-oriented probers typically
paired with advanced thermal-control solutions, we expect HTSI's thermal-module
shipments to reach 3,000 units in 2027E from 1,200 in 2026E. Combined with testing,
installation and service revenue, this should drive HTSI sales growth of 127%/152% in
2026E/27E.
Accelerators still in focus with TPU as another significant growth driver

Nvidia remains a key growth driver for both CHPT and WinWay. We expect both
companies to gain share with Rubin, with WinWay's socket share improving from
roughly 50% historically to 60% with Rubin, while CHPT is positioned to supply around
70% of probe card and final test PCB content. Nvidia currently accounts for roughly
50% of WinWay's revenue and 30% of CHPT's.
Beyond Nvidia, we view Google's TPU roadmap as a meaningful incremental
opportunity. Following the successful ramp of TPU v8t with MediaTek as design partner,

CHPT is positioned to supply probe card PCBs, final test load boards, system-level test
boards and burn-in boards. WinWay will supply sockets for both TPU v8t and v9, with
the larger v9 package providing another growth leg. We estimate production of 4mn
TPU v8t units in 2027E and 2.3mn TPU v9 packages (9.2mn dies) alongside 2m v8t units
in 2028E.
CPU business as a supplemental catalyst for test interface in an agentic
world
With agentic AI proliferation, CPU demand is increasing significantly and this directly

correlates to increasing demand for testing consumables. WinWay and CHPT should
both benefit from the strong ramp up of Nvidia's ARM based Vera CPU and Google's
ARM based Axion CPU in 2026E and 2027E. WinWay will be supplying sockets and
probe cards for Vera and Axion, and CHPT will be the probe card and PCB supplier for
both Vera and Axion. WinWay will have the additional upside from AMD's x86 Venice
CPU, and CHPT could capture some share through AMD's equipment vendor

procurement. We estimate CHPT's CPU related sales to be low to mid-teens % of total
sales in 2026E and growing to 20-30% in 2027E  with CHPT's share expansions with
turnkey probe card solutions for Google's Axion. For WinWay, we believe CPU should
account for around 30% of sales in 2026E/27E.

### Page 4

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 4
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor testing
ficonTEC's 25 years of precision optical-test expertise makes it an early leader in CPO test
equipment, particularly in optical alignment, a critical capability for high-volume
manufacturing. Through its partnership with ficonTEC, HTSI is well positioned to
benefit from the initial CPO ramp in 2027E-28E by supporting manufacturing scale-up.

We expect both insertion 2 and insertion 3 testing to be required over the next two
years. Despite the technical challenges of double-sided wafer probing, insertion 2
testing remains important given current optical-engine yields and the need to identify
known-good optical engines before fibre-array-unit (FAU) coupling. This becomes
increasingly critical as switch designs integrate larger numbers of optical engines.
We estimate HTSI will ship 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E , with

further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands and next-generation platforms move
into volume production.
Testing TAM expanding with IC complexity: leaders likely to
outperform industry growth
Our central view is that testing content per AI accelerator is growing faster than
semiconductor unit volumes, creating a multi-year expansion in testing TAM. We believe
semiconductor testing is entering a period of structural growth as AI-driven chip
complexity increases testing intensity across wafer, package and system levels. The

industry's shift-left strategy, which moves more testing upstream in the semiconductor
manufacturing process, is resulting in longer and more frequent test insertions. As cloud
AI GPUs and ASICs become increasingly complex, additional testing is required to ensure
functionality, performance and yield, while reducing defective parts per million (DPPM).
More robust testing should improve deployment efficiency and lower the cost of
operating AI infrastructure.

Semiconductor testing generally comprises four stages: wafer/chip probing (CP), final
test (FT), system-level test (SLT), and burn-in. CP is conducted at the wafer-level before
packaging, while FT, SLT, and burn-in occur after packaging. The below Figure 3S e m i c o n d u c t o r  t e s t i n g  p r o c e s s :  f r o m  w a f e r  l e v e l  t o  p a c k a g e  l e v e l
illustrates the semiconductor manufacturing flow.
Figure 3: Semiconductor testing process: from wafer level to package level

Wafer Manufacturing Wafer Probing Packaging Testing
Burn-in
Final Test (FT)
System-Level Test (SLT)
Source: UBS
We estimate semiconductor testing historically accounts for ~1% of total
semiconductor revenue. Within testing, consumables such as probe cards, sockets and
interface boards represent ~10-15% of industry revenue. According to Yole, the probe
card, socket and interface board markets should grow from US$3.8bn, US$2.3bn and
US$1.7bn in 2026 to US$5.5bn, US$3.1bn and US$2.0bn by 2031, respectively.

Nonetheless, leaders within the testing sector are likely to stand out among competition
with greater sales CAGR comparing to the industry. Below we highlight Hermes

drivers and how this compares with broader industry sales CAGR.
We see HTSI's sales for the next few years to mainly  be driven by 1) thermal control
module shipment and 2) CPO equipment shipment. In the past, thermal control

modules weren't necessary for chip probing, due to less TDP for the chips. However, as
new iterations of AI accelerators now have substantial power draw, this unlocks a brand
new TAM for HTSI to enter with their thermal control modules to pair with probers, in
order to prevent probe card damage.
 Robust thermal control module shipments.  We estimate HTSI to ship

future years,  bringing their 2026-31E semiconductor equipment sales CAGR to

48%.

 Demand supported by Advantest expansions. We believe the 3,000 thermal
control modules shipment forecast for 2027E is not far-fetched, as we read-across
from Advantest's announced capacity expansion plan (testers will be paired with
probers with a 1:1 ratio) with increasing capacity up to 5,000 testers within

FY2027/3 and they also announced a 10,000-unit target within CY2028.

see a good portion to be allocated to chip probing given the shift-left testing trend
increasing both testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CPO is another brand new TAM for semiconductor testing.  As insertion 2
and 3 testing equipment demand starts to rise in 2027E with scale-out CPO

adoption, we expect HTSI to ship 40/70 insertion 2 and 3 equipment in 2027E ,

proliferates, and driving 52% 2026-31E test equipment & engineering services
sales CAGR.
 CPO to see content increase. Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment may start to ship in
2027E, with higher throughput and tool ASP .
 Insertion 2 might scale down, but not likely  any time soon.  Insertion 2
equipment shipment might come down in 2029E and beyond, as foundries scale-

up yield for photonic IC (PIC) in order to lessen the time consuming insertion 2
testing burden.

The AI era has materially increased the value of semiconductor testing. Compared with
traditional mobile and automotive chips, AI accelerators incorporate larger compute
dies, more advanced nodes, chiplet architectures, advanced packaging technologies,
multiple I/O dies, and increasing HBM content. These factors drive higher-value test

consumables, including advanced MEMS probe cards, multilayer interface boards, and
next-generation sockets designed to support higher pin counts, thermal loading, and
power delivery requirements.
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC applications, are set to increase
in both front-end wafer fabrication advancements and back-end packaging
complexities with each iterations (i.e. Nvidia's roadmap from Blackwell to Rubin to
Feynman or Google's TPU roadmap from v7 to v8 to v9).   This trend is evident with

TSMC's front-end  and TSMC, Intel, and OSAT's back-end advanced packaging capacity
expansions. For front-end, we see industry N3 capacity expanding from 156kwpm in
year end 2025 to 224/283/293kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E, and N2 capacity from
47kwpm in year-end 2025 to 111/200/312kpwm in year end 2026E/27E/28E. For back-
end, we are seeing industry CoWoS capacity rising from 90kwpm in 2025 to 160kwpm
in 2026E and further expanding to 270kwpm in 2027E.

Final-test, system-level test, and burn-in content should continue to increase as AI
accelerators become larger, more power-hungry and more complex. Rising thermal

design power and packaging complexity increase validation requirements, driving
longer test times and higher demand for test equipment to prevent testing from
becoming a production bottleneck.

While architectural changes can affect test requirements for individual product
generations, we expect the long-term trend of rising testing content to remain intact.
For example, Nvidia's Rubin Ultra adopts a simpler package structure than previously
expected, from a 4-die structure to a 2-die structure and Rubin Ultra HBM de-spec from

HBM4E 12-Hi to initially use HBM4 8-Hi and upgrade to HBM4E 8-Hi at the latter stages,
which may moderate near-term test growth. However, future products such as Nvidia's
Feynman are expected to reintroduce greater package complexity, with a four-GPU per
package structure. More importantly, any reduction in test time can be offset by higher
production volumes, making throughput requirements equally important for testing
demand.

Below we summarize key technical items of probe cards, sockets, and load boards/PCB
used for semiconductor testing:
Probe cards and chip probing (CP)
Probe cards are used during chip probing (CP), the wafer-level stage of semiconductor

testing that identifies known-good dies (KGD) before packaging. As advanced
packaging technologies such as CoWoS, 3D stacking and hybrid bonding become more
prevalent, CP is becoming increasingly important because a single defective die or HBM
stack can compromise the entire package. This is driving the industry's shift-left testing
strategy, with more testing conducted earlier in the manufacturing process to identify
defects before costly packaging steps.

Probe cards consist of contact elements (probes) mounted on a PCB and are used
together with probers and automated test equipment (ATE). During testing, wafers are
placed on the prober chuck while the probe card makes electrical contact with individual
dies. The tester then performs electrical, parametric, and functional tests to determine
whether each die meets performance requirements. Dies that fail testing are removed
from the production flow prior to dicing and packaging.

Furthermore, probe cards are highly customised products designed for specific chip
architectures and testing platforms. As AI accelerators adopt larger dies, higher pin
counts and more advanced packaging technologies, probe card complexity and content
value continue to increase.

### Page 9

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 9

cards to find known-good dies (KGD) and
eliminate faulty dies

The global probe card market remains concentrated among a handful of suppliers:
 FormFactor (FORM.O): World's largest probe card company (including both logic
and memory probe cards) by revenue as of year end 2025. Key supplier for HBM

probe cards.
 Technoprobe (TPRO.MI): World's largest logic probe card company by revenue
as of year end 2025. Key supplier for AI accelerators MEMS probe cards, especially
for GPU.
 MJC (6871.T): Japanese company, mainly specializing in memory probe cards, 3rd
largest probe card company by revenue as of year end 2025.
 MPI (6223.TWO) : Traditionally specializes in cantilever  probe cards, now
expanding its MEMS probe capacity. 4th largest probe card company by revenue as

of year end 2025. A probe card supplier of AI ASICs.
 JEM (6855.T): Mainly specialize in memory probe cards, 5th largest probe card
company by revenue as of year end 2025.
 WinWay (6515.TWO): One of the fastest-growing logic probe card suppliers.
Following its collaboration with Technoprobe, WinWay rose from the 15th-largest
probe card supplier in 2024 to the fifth-largest logic probe card supplier in 2025.
Probe card revenue increased from 11% of sales in 2024 to 29% in 2025.

 Chunghwa Precision Test Tech (6510.TW) : A key supplier for smartphone
application processor (AP) probe cards, and has been migrating to HPC related
applications in recent years. Although its share within the probe card market is
minor, it is a major supplier for probe card PCB, especially for high end probe cards
with advanced PCB that has high layer counts (>40 layers, and over 80 layers for AI
applications). Its key competitive advantage is its vertical integration as it

manufacturers the MEMS probe and PCB in-house.

Probe cards generally employ either vertical or cantilever architectures. Vertical probe
cards use probes arranged perpendicular to the substrate, enabling higher pin density

### Page 10

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 10
and finer pitch capability. They have become the standard for AI, HPC and other
advanced semiconductor applications. Cantilever probe cards use angled probes
extending from the PCB and are typically used in analog, driver IC and selected memory
applications where pin-count and pitch requirements are less demanding. The latest
generation of AI accelerators increasingly relies on MEMS (Micro-Electro-Mechanical

Systems) based vertical probe cards, which support ultra-fine pitches, higher pin counts
and improved probing precision. Technoprobe currently leads this segment with pitch
capability reaching approximately 40 micrometres.
For leading-edge AI applications, probe cards must increasingly support:
1. Higher current-carrying capability (CCC).
2. Stable contact resistance.
3. Lower parasitic inductance for signal integrity.
4. Greater durability under varying thermal conditions.

For CHPT, with their MEMS probe capabilities stemming from their smartphone
application processor probe card supply, they are in a good position to capture the
growing demand for MEMS probe cards as AI/HPC are shifting to MEMS probes. We also
expect probe card content per device to continue rising as AI accelerators grow in die
size, pin count, power consumption and packaging complexity. This should drive secular
growth for both probe card suppliers and upstream component providers, making chip

probing one of the most attractive areas within the semiconductor testing value chain.

Sockets
Semiconductor test sockets are electro-mechanical interfaces that are used post chip
probing and chip packaging process to test the functionality of the chips. Sockets are

placed on to load boards on top of testers during the final test/system level test
processes. Handlers place the chips onto the sockets, and pins inside the sockets enable
the testers to read the signals from the device under test (DUT, i.e. the packaged chips),
with tester signals fanning out  from beneath the PCB load board.

As AI accelerators become larger and more complex, socket requirements continue to
increase. High-end sockets typically comprise a socket housing, alignment plate,
integrated thermal-control components and contact elements that connect the DUT to
the tester. Spring probes (pogo pins) are the most common choice for HPC sockets, with
high durability, individual probe replacement flexibility, and consistent contact force. FT

and SLT sockets account for more than 70% of the total socket market, with burn-in
sockets representing the remainder.
Compared with the probe card market, the socket industry is more fragmented. Key
suppliers include:
 WinWay (6515.TW) : A major share gainer in AI/HPC sockets, with strong
exposure to Nvidia and AMD. The company specializes in high-performance
coaxial sockets and is developing its next-generation HyperSocket platform. It is
also expanding into probe cards through its partnership with Technoprobe.

 Leeno (058470.KQ) : Vertically integrated supplier focused on smartphone AP
and memory sockets.
 ISC (095340.KQ): Leading memory socket supplier with elastomer expertise.
 Smiths Interconnect (Acquired by Molex) : A key player in AI/HPC socket
industry, with its DaVinci coaxial sockets able to support high frequency
connectivity testing.
 Yamaichi (6941.T): Leading Japanese socket supplier with strong automotive
exposure.
 Yokowo (6800.T): Major provider of contact probes and socket pins.

 Cohu (COHU.O) : Broad test-solutions provider spanning ATE, handlers and
sockets, with greater auto/industrial/consumer  exposure.

AI accelerators are pushing socket requirements beyond 10,000 pins, making coaxial

sockets the industry standard for leading-edge AI/HPC devices. These sockets must
support transmission speeds of up to 224G while handling significantly higher thermal
design power (TDP) during testing.
Elastomer technology is increasingly being adopted alongside spring-probe (pogo-pin)
architectures to reduce chip damage and improve contact reliability. WinWay's next-
generation HyperSocket integrates elastomer materials between the DUT and spring

probes, improving probe lifetime, reducing cleaning requirements and enhancing
production efficiency. We believe HyperSocket could become an important
differentiator as next-generation AI accelerators demand higher pin counts, bandwidth
and thermal performance. We estimate HyperSocket could contribute approximately
5% of WinWay's revenue in 2027E while carrying a 20-30% ASP premium versus
conventional coaxial sockets.
The transition toward larger AI packages, higher pin counts and faster data-transfer

speeds should continue to increase socket content per device. We expect this trend to
support long-term growth in the socket market, with technology leaders such as
WinWay well positioned to gain share through next-generation solutions including

HyperSocket.

Load Board/Interface board
Load boards are specialised printed circuit boards (PCBs) that connect the tester to the
device under test (DUT) during final test (FT) and system-level test (SLT). Acting as the
electrical interface between the packaged chip and automated test equipment (ATE),

load boards are responsible for power delivery and high-speed signal transmission
throughout the testing process. As AI and HPC devices become increasingly complex,
load board requirements continue to rise. Multilayer construction is crucial as modern
load boards often require more than 50 layers for smartphone application processors
and over 80 layers for leading-edge AI accelerators to support higher pin counts, dense
signal routing and greater power delivery demands. At the same time, impedance

control is an important factor as maintaining signal integrity is becoming increasingly
important as interconnect bandwidth rises and high-speed SerDes blocks become more
prevalent. These trends are driving greater technology content and value per load board.
We expect load board content to increase alongside AI chip complexity, larger package
sizes and higher interface speeds, benefiting suppliers with advanced multilayer PCB
capabilities such as CHPT.

Figure 22: CHPT's AP load board
Source: Company data (CHPT)
Figure 23: CHPT's AI load board
Source: Company data (CHPT)
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing
The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging. This

### Page 13

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 13
underpins our above-consensus forecasts for Chunghwa Precision Test (CHPT), with
2027E/28E sales 7%/15% above consensus driven by structural AI/HPC probe card and
test consumables demand, capacity expansion, and increasing test interface content as
test insertions and test time increase. WinWay should also benefit through its growing
probe card business alongside partner Technoprobe, with probe card sales expected to

grow 94%/72% YoY in 2026E/27E following 161%/260% growth in 2024/25.
Figure 24: CP is set to grow substantially with  shift-left, each packaging step set to be MORE costly than the prior step
RDL / RWAT testing Wafer-level CP (WLCP)

oS
Source: UBS
The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while

defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed
testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million

(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall
integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of

wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected to surge  another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level chip probe
(WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and higher power
densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield degradation,
creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal control solutions.

Hermes Testing (HTSI) is a key beneficiary of this trend. Leveraging its long-standing
relationship with TSMC and expertise in semiconductor test solutions, HTSI supplies
advanced thermal control modules used alongside leading-edge probers (made by
Tokyo Electron), particularly for AI applications. These thermal control modules are

### Page 14

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 14
required for high TDP semiconductor wafer probe in order to maintain the temperature
of the chuck to avoid needle burns or probe card damages. We estimate HTSI will ship
approximately 3,000 thermal-management modules in 2027E, up from 1,200 in 2026E,
supporting sales growth of 127%/152% in 2026E/27E. HTSI is also the partner for
prober installation, prober maintenance and services. In addition, HTSI provides high-

end chuck processing services, an area that should benefit from increasing package sizes
and higher wafer warpage associated with advanced packaging.
Beyond thermal control modules, HTSI also supplies consumables and customised
solutions for wafer-level testing, including probe card cleaning sheets and laser-based
probe head cleaning systems. As probing intensity increases under the shift-left testing
model, demand for cleaning and maintenance solutions should rise alongside probe

card utilisation. Currently, cleaning sheets is around 5% of sales,  but we believe it could
gain traction in 2027/28E and beyond. We see additional upside from future modular
laser cleaning systems that could be integrated directly with probers, further expanding
HTSI's content opportunity within advanced wafer-level testing.
Rapid capacity expansions accelerating growth
Industry test interface suppliers are expanding capacity aggressively to meet rising AI

and HPC demand. As advanced semiconductors require higher-performance probe
cards, sockets and interface boards, capacity additions are becoming increasingly
important to support growth across both wafer-level and package-level testing.
We believe CHPT and WinWay are well positioned to gain share within the high-end AI/
HPC test interface market. Taiwanese suppliers benefit from their proximity to leading
fabless customers and the growing preference for localised supply chains, particularly

for advanced testing applications where responsiveness and engineering support are
critical. Against a backdrop of tight industry supply, both companies have announced
significant capacity expansion plans over the next two to three years.
CHPT: capacity expansion supporting AI/HPC share gains
CHPT's existing capacity is effectively fully utilised and the company is pursuing both
near-term and long-term expansion. Near-term growth will come from leased facilities,

with one production expansion scheduled for August 2026 and a second in March
2027, doubling and tripling year-end 2025 capacity, respectively. Longer term, CHPT's
new Fab 3 facility in Pingzhen will significantly expand in-house PCB manufacturing and
wet-processing capacity, with mass production expected around Q428, expected to
double existing capacity when fully ramped.
Combined with increasing exposure to higher-value AI/HPC products, including turnkey

CPU probe cards and TPU FT/SLT/burn-in boards, we expect CHPT to deliver revenue
growth of 49%/75%/43% in 2026E/27E/28E.
WinWay: scaling for the next phase of AI demand
WinWay's capacity is expected to increase from 3.5mn pins per month at year-end 2025
to 6mn currently, reaching 9mn by year-end 2026 and 14mn by year-end 2027 through
expansions in Renwu, Kaohsiung. By 2027, approximately 60% of pogo-pin
requirements should be internally sourced, strengthening both supply security and

margins.
We estimate these capacity additions will support revenue growth of 105%/73%/71%
in 2026E/27E/28E.

Advanced packaging growth remains the key demand driver
Capacity expansion by test interface vendors is ultimately supported by continued

investment throughout the advanced packaging ecosystem, from providers such as
TSMC, ASE, Intel, and Amkor. During the Q226 earnings calls, TSMC raised its recent
2026 capex from US$52-56bn to US$60-64bn and ASE raised its 2026 capex from US
$8.5bn to US$10.5bn, with both companies citing robust demand supporting the capex
raise. We believe capex for the next two years will be even higher than 2026, implying
continuous growth in future years. With advanced packaging technologies getting

significantly more complex and with larger form factor, illustrated by the upcoming
ramp of both TSMC's CoPoS (chip-on-panel-on-substrate) and Intel's EMIB-T
(embedded multi-die interconnect bridge) packaging technologies, test interface
solution providers will benefit with more value added consumables (see our CoPoS vs
EMIB-T deep dive).
We expect test interface content growth to outpace unit growth. For instance, test
socket's ASP directly correlates with its pin counts - with the addition of 20-30% more

pins inside the sockets, socket ASP can grow 20-30% or more as the socket might
require significant cooling capabilities as the TDP of the DUT rises with its larger form
factor. Unlike prior semiconductor cycles, much of the current demand is driven by rising
test interface content per device rather than unit growth alone.

Accelerators still in focus with TPU as another significant
growth driver
Nvidia has been the primary driver of testing content growth over the past several years,
benefiting the entire semiconductor testing supply chain. We expect this trend to
continue as GPU architectures evolve from Blackwell to Rubin and then to Feynman,
with increasing process node complexity, larger package sizes and more advanced

packaging technologies driving higher testing value per device.
Nvidia should remain a major contributor to both CHPT and WinWay, with GPUs
continuing to serve as their core growth engine. Both companies are positioned to gain
share with Rubin. We estimate WinWay's socket share could improve from a 50/50 split
with Smiths Interconnect in prior generations to 60/40 with Rubin, while CHPT is gaining

### Page 16

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 16
share in test interface PCBs and is positioned to supply around 70% of Rubin probe card
and final test PCB content. Nvidia currently accounts for approximately 50% of
WinWay's sales and 30% of CHPT's sales. Both Nvidia GPUs and Google TPUs increase
testing demand through higher content per device rather than unit growth alone.
Beyond Nvidia, Google's TPU roadmap represents a significant incremental growth

opportunity. Following the successful ramp of TPU v8t with MediaTek as design partner,
CHPT is positioned to supply probe card PCBs, final test load boards, system-level test
boards and burn-in boards. For v9, although the final test interface vendors are yet to be
decided, we believe as the current supplier, CHPT will still be supplying PCB for final test,
system level test, and burn-in, with potential to continue to supply PCB for probe cards.
WinWay should also benefit as the socket supplier for both TPU v8t and the next-

generation TPU v9 platform. We believe MediaTek's increasing share gain for Google
TPUs will also benefit the local Taiwanese test interface supply chain.
We view TPU v9 as a particularly attractive opportunity given its large four-die package
architecture based on Intel's EMIB-T packaging technology, which should drive higher
testing content. We estimate production of 4mn TPU v8t units in 2027E, followed by
2.3mn TPU v9 packages (9.2mn dies) and an additional 2mn TPU v8t units in 2028E.

Importantly, while EMIB-T packaging is expected to be performed in Malaysia, we
believe the majority of advanced testing value should remain within the Taiwanese
testing ecosystem. Leading testing providers, including ASE and KYEC, maintain
substantial testing operations across Taiwan and Southeast Asia, supporting continued
participation by Taiwanese test interface suppliers. See our latest MediaTek report.
CPU business as a supplemental catalyst for test interface in

an agentic world
We believe semiconductor test interfaces are an underappreciated beneficiary of the
emerging agentic AI ecosystem. UBS estimates the CPU market will expand to US
$170bn by 2030E from US$31bn in 2025 and US$71bn in 2027E, driven by rising
deployment of AI infrastructure. As CPU-to-GPU ratios increasingly approach 1:1 in AI
clusters, demand for testing interfaces should expand alongside CPU volumes. TSMC is
the main CP test provider in the industry, with probe card suppliers aiding its massive

demand by supplying probes, such as TSMC's GPU probe cards supported by
Technoprobe's probes. However, with capacity now becoming scarce with strong front
and back-end demand, TSMC is outsourcing its CP business to ASE and KYEC, further
broadening the testing supply chain. This is where new probe card vendors come in—
smaller probe card players in the past, such as  WinWay and CHPT, now have the
opportunity to gain significant, non-GPU share from TSMC, as they have MEMS probe

capabilities (WinWay's MEMS probes are from its strategic partnership with
Technoprobe) and has a long established relationship with the fabless companies such as
Nvidia via supplying non-probe card consumables such as sockets and PCB. Of which,
CPU projects have become the main area for CHPT and WinWay to expand their
footprint.
We expect both companies to benefit from the ramp of Nvidia's ARM-based Vera CPU
and Google's ARM-based Axion CPU in 2026E-27E. WinWay is positioned to supply

sockets and probe cards for Vera and sockets for Axion, while CHPT should provide
probe cards and test interface PCBs for both Vera and Axion, with particularly strong
exposure to Axion. Additional upside could come from AMD's Venice CPU, where
WinWay is well positioned and CHPT could gain indirect exposure through testing-
equipment procurement channels. We estimate CPU-related revenue will represent low
to mid-teens % of CHPT sales in 2026E, increasing to 20-30% in 2027E as the company

expands its turnkey probe card solutions for Axion. For WinWay, CPU-related demand
could account for approximately 30% of sales in 2026E-27E . Importantly, CPU
programmes diversify exposure beyond AI GPUs and provide a second structural growth
driver for test interface suppliers.
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor
testing
As interconnect bottlenecks increasingly shift from the memory wall to the copper wall,
we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) adoption is inevitable over the longer term. Testing

remains one of the key bottlenecks to CPO deployment, as optical validation differs

### Page 17

Asia Semiconductor Insight 8 October 2026 ab 17
fundamentally from the electrical testing processes that have dominated semiconductor
manufacturing for decades. As a result, CPO is creating a new testing opportunity across
the semiconductor equipment and consumables ecosystem. We have written
extensively on CPO and the opportunities that it brings to the semiconductor supply
chain, see our CPO deep dive , our thoughts on CPO timing ,  2026 SiPhIA conference

takeaways, and SEMICON 2026 takeaways.
Various test equipment suppliers are scrambling to come up with ideal testing
equipment across the four insertions of CPO testing, with solutions to ensure both
optical and electrical signal integrity of the CPO solutions. The four insertions are:
 Insertion 1: Electronic IC (EIC)/Photonic IC (PIC) test.  Single-side wafer test
that has dedicated probe cards to test the fabricated EIC/PIC wafers.
 Insertion 2: Optical/Electrical test. Double sided wafer test, testing the hybrid

bonded (e.g. via TSMC's COUPE) EIC and PIC wafers by an opto-electrical test that
verifies the electrical signals on the top and optical signal on the bottom of the
hybrid bonded wafers.
 Insertion 3: Optical engine (OE) test.  The OE tests are done to ensure the
quality of the OEs and with FAU (Fiber Array Unit) attached, to ensure the signal
integrity of the OEs. The testing equipment needs to be able to handle 224G per
channel data rates, requiring high unit per hour (UPH) throughput, meticulous dirt

mitigation, and maintaining a near 100% contact rating on high-speed sockets.
 Insertion 4: ASIC final test.  This final test step is established post the OEs are
packaged together with the ASIC. The complete ASIC package (now with CPO) is
now tested for opto-electrical functionalities.
As it stands, insertion 2 and insertion 3 are the two areas where there are the most
debates in the industry. Insertion 3 suppliers such as Chroma have noted the trend of

“shift-to-middle” paradigm centered on insertion 3. In high-density scale-out switches
deploying up to 32 optical engines (e.g. Nvidia’s Spectrum-X architectures), any OE
defect results in package scrap at the CoWoS level, making 100% known-good die
(KGD) validation at the singulated OE stage an absolute commercial requirement.

With its 25 years of precision test equipment expertise, ficonTEC is an early mover for
CPO test equipment and has key know-how for optical alignment, which is
quintessential for optical test. Hermes Testing, through its partnership with ficonTEC,
is set to be a major beneficiary once CPO proliferates in 2027E-28E. As the partner of
ficonTEC, Hermes Testing is the key enabler that can help ficonTEC achieve HVM for its

CPO testing equipment. As of now, our view is that both insertion 2 and insertion 3
should be required within the next two years, despite the difficulties surrounding
double-sided wafer probing (insertion 2), as the yield for OEs is still not high enough to
avoid defective dies. Finding out known-good OEs will be crucial before the FAU
coupling process during the latter stages of CPO packaging, to prevent impeding the
final package with multiple OEs attached to the switch ASIC (e.g. 32 OEs on Nvidia's

Spectrum-X). We estimate Hermes Testing will ship 40/70 units of insertion 2/3 testing
equipment in 2027E, with upside to these numbers as we view Hermes Testing will likely
assist with the ramp up for Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment in 2027E. Gen 2 insertion
equipment will come with better throughput and ASP .

Beyond insertions 2 and 3, HTSI is also positioned to participate in insertion 1 testing
through customised prober solutions for photonic integrated circuit (PIC) testing. We
believe these solutions could eventually be packaged as licensable intellectual property,
creating an additional long-term growth opportunity as CPO adoption broadens. While
near-term testing growth is driven by AI accelerators, CPO represents the next major

expansion cycle for semiconductor testing. We believe HTSI is uniquely positioned to
participate across multiple test insertions, providing exposure to a potentially significant
source of incremental revenue beginning in 2027E.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Tech investing involves risk. It is difficult for the investment community, UBS included, to
project the financial results of tech companies, as their operating models are highly volatile
and unpredictable, and they compete in a highly dynamic marketplace. In addition to their
low predictability, valuing tech stocks can be challenging because neither traditional nor non-

traditional valuation measures have provided much insight into how tech stocks trade.
We value Chroma ATE using a PE methodology.
Downside risks include weaker-than-expected demand for high-performance computing
dampening its semiconductor testing business and slower-than-expected recovery in EV
investments into battery capacity installations.
We value Chunghwa Precision Test, Hermes Testing and WinWay based on PE methodology.
Risks: Faster- or slower-than-expected front-end/CoWoS capacity expansion could result in

CHPT, HTSI and WinWay gaining/losing probe card/board orders from major foundries and
OSATs. Potential risk of competition (from either local or foreign companies) achieving
technological advancements could result in these companies losing market share from end
customers, major foundries, and OSATs. Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could lead
to a ramp-up or a slowdown of product orders from customers, major foundries, and OSATs
to CHPT, HTSI and WinWay, affecting long-term revenue growth potential. A potential

slowdown or delay of the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as
SoIC, CoPoS, and even CPO, would limit future growth potential. Rising raw material costs
may directly impact their margins for equipment shipments.
We value Hon Precision based on PE multiple.
Faster- or slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion could indirectly result in Hon
Precision gaining or losing equipment orders from major customers; the risk of competition

from either local or foreign companies achieving technological advancements could result in
Hon Precision losing market share from its major customers; better- or worse-than-expected
AI growth could lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from customers to
Hon Precision, affecting long-term revenue growth potential; a potential slowdown or delay
in the rollout of technological advancements for technologies, such as MCL and CPO, would
limit future equipment orders; rising raw material costs may directly impact Hon Precision’s

margins for equipment shipments.
